the remaining inputs of P:static-phase-all-orders for the order bounds: entireness in the coupling, the coefficient identification, the band and the cutoff removals (see that row)
the free estimate ||S_0(-+h)||_lambda <= sqrt(e), the group law S_0(-h) S_0(h) = 1, Z_0 = S_0(h)(f,g) and the norm comparisons ||Z_n||_lambda <= (lambda h)^(-1) ||Z_n||_(1/h), ||(f,g)||_(1/h) <= ||(f,g)||_lambda (lines 3273-3292); an abstract complete real inner product space stands for L^2(Omega; X_lambda)
Gaussian symmetry, E<Y_r, Y_s>_lambda = 0 for r + s odd (line 3293)
the first-variance bounds of P:static-phase-variance, E||Y_1||^2 <= e E:phase-born and |E<Y_0, Y_2>| <= N sqrt(e) E:phase-mean (lines 3275-3280)
for E:independent-static-blocks, the per-block bound ||I_j y||^2 <= (1 + E(h_j, sigma_j, lambda)) ||y||^2 on phases adapted to the past (E:static-interaction with density and tensoring with the independent past, lines 3333-3337), adaptation being an abstract predicate preserved by each block, and ||S_0(h_j)||^2 <= e^(lambda h_j)		 Wrappers static_interaction_completed_paper and independent_static_blocks_operator_paper state R_alpha, D_Gamma by E:phase-constants under the class of S:static-phase (only 0 < alpha < 2 is used; local finiteness is carried); the identification I = sum sigma^n Y_n and the per-block step are proved. E:static-block-series is proved with nothing supplied.
